The [Magic Online Championship Series](/en/articles/feature/2009/05-07-magic-online-championship-series) awards invites to the [Magic World Championships](https://web.archive.org/web/20090831000000/https://magic.wizards.com/en/node/642286) as well as a cash tournament for players who qualify through the series. The 2009 Season 5 Championship was held on August 30. The format was M10 sealed deck with a Top 8 M10 draft. Click below to launch the draft viewer to see how they drafted.

[](https://web.archive.org/web/20090831000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=m10-532377)

### [Launch the Draft Viewer](https://web.archive.org/web/20090831000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=m10-532377)

Here's how the top 8 matches occurred.

**Tormented, 2-1** 

Fwesh, 2-0 

### Finals

**Anathik, 2-1** 

Tormented, 2-0 

### Champion

**Anathik, 2-1**
